Algorithm `power_kkt_iter(a, q, iters, alpha0, alpha1, blend)` :
- Current a normalized.
- For it in range:
   c=conv(a)

   Build operator A(p).
   Solve regularized LSQR with b=[ones, sqrtα*a_prev], alpha decays.
   a_s = solution.
   Project: a_new = np.maximum(a_s,0). Normalize. If all zero, fallback.
   Maybe blend: a = (1-blend)*a + blend*a_new; normalize? Or set a=a_new. Blend may maintain positivity. Use acceptance: compute exact score q objective? Original score=2n max c. If original improves, update best; for iteration current a can be accepted even if original slightly worse? We can keep best overall separately, current maybe best. If raw a_new score worse than current, try blend with old: 0.5. Choose best among old, new, blends. This is local search.
   Rescale to sum1.

For Hankel operator:
```
class HankelOp:
    def __init__(self,p,n):
       self.n=n; self.p=p; self.M=nextpow2(3*n-2); self.Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,self.M)
    def matvec(self,v):
       m=self.n
       r=v[::-1]
       conv=np.fft.irfft(self.Fp*np.fft.rfft(r,self.M), self.M)
       return conv[m-1:2*m-1]
    def rmatvec(self,w): return self.matvec(w) # symmetric
```
But for RegularOp with alpha,a_prev:
```
class RegularHankelOp:
    def __init__(self,p,n,alpha,a_prev):
       self.hank=HankelOp(p,n); self.n=n; self.alpha=alpha; self.sqrt_alpha=np.sqrt(alpha); self.a_prev=a_prev
       self.shape=(2*n,n); self.dtype=np.float64
    def matvec(self,v):
       av=self.hank.matvec(v)
       return np.concatenate((av, self.sqrt_alpha*v))
    def rmatvec(self,w):
       w1=w[:self.n]; w2=w[self.n:]
       av=self.hank.rmatvec(w1)
       return av + self.alpha*w2
```
LSQR expects LinearOperator with shape and dtype. We can set attributes. It might also call `A.dot`? OK.

```
def make_weights(c, mode, beta/power, top_k, mix_uniform):
    cm = max(c)
    if mode == 'lse': logw=beta*(c-cm); clip; w=exp; sum
    elif 'power': wh = c/cm; w=wh^power; sum
    elif 'top': w zeros, top indices set 1
    if mix: w = (1-mix)*w + mix/L
```

Now implement `fast_conv` carefully:
```
def conv_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    m = 2*n -1
    M = 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, M)[:m]
    return c
```
For nonnegative a, c nonnegative but numerical small negative? c can be tiny negative due floating? Convolution of nonnegative with same should be nonnegative, but FFT error maybe -1e-16. We can clip if needed for scores: `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)` for weights? But for exact max, small negative no issue. Could clip in score to 0. `max` unaffected. For weights, negative would produce issues; clip.

Normalization:
```
def normalize_pos(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.full(len(a),1/len(a))
    return a/s
```

```

def hankel_matvec_fixed(p, n, M, Fp, v):
    r=v[::-1]
    fr=np.fft.rfft(r, M)
    return np.fft.irfft(Fp*fr, M)[n-1:2*n-1]
```
But for speed, in matvec we need Fp. Could implement class:
```
class HankelMatvec:
    def __init__(self, p, n):
       self.n=n; self.p=p
       L=2*n-1; M=1 << ((L+n-2)-1).bit_length() # length 3n-2
       self.M=M
       self.Fp=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    def apply(self, v):
       n=self.n
       r=v[::-1]
       fr=np.fft.rfft(r, self.M)
       return np.fft.irfft(self.Fp*fr, self.M)[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Use `m1=n-1`, slice `m1:2*n-1` length n. Check slice end: for n=2, n-1=1, 2n-1=3 -> [1:3] length2 okay. General end=2n-1, start=n-1 -> length n. Good.

Can we make `make_weights` with q/power produce p stable for c? Since c sum1. For q large, c^(q-1) underflow. Use log:
```
def weights_from_c(c, mode, beta=None, power=None, topk=None, mix=0.0):
    L=len(c); cm=max(c)
    w=np.zeros(L)
    if mode=='lse':
        if beta is None: beta = 1.0
        scaled=beta*(c-cm)
        scaled=np.clip(scaled, -50, 0)
        w=np.exp(scaled)
    elif mode=='power':
        if power is None: power=2
        # to avoid underflow: logw=power*log(c/cm)
        r=np.maximum(c, 1e-300)/cm
        logw=power*np.log(r)
        logw=np.clip(logw, -50, 0)
        w=np.exp(logw)
    elif mode in ('top','topmix'):
        idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
        w[idx]=1.0
    wsum=w.sum()
    if wsum <=0: w=np.ones(L)/L
    else: w/=wsum
    if mix>0: w=(1-mix)*w + mix*(1/L)
    return w
```
For LSE, beta in terms of c scale. Since c~1/n, to get meaningful exp, beta ~n. We can set beta schedule e.g. n*0.1, n*1, n*10, n*100. For power, power=1,2,4,8,16.

def power_subgrad(a, power, steps, eta0, decay, f_lb):
   a=normalize
   v=zeros
   best_score=score
   for it in steps:
      c=conv(a)
      p=weights(c, mode='power', power=power)
      G=2*hankel_apply(p,a)
      Gx=G - a*dot(a,G)
      gnorm=norm(Gx)
      sc=score
      eta=min(eta, (sc - lowerbound+epsilon)/gnorm^2?) or fixed
      v = 0.9*v - eta*Gx
      trial = normalize(clip(a+v,0))
      if score(trial)<best: update
      else maybe v scaled
```

Pseudo-code for `kkt_cycle`:
```
def kkt_cycle(a, deadline, modes, max_steps, alpha_start, alpha_min, decay, max_iter, mix_start, mix_end, line_lambdas, tol):
    current = normalize_pos(a)
    sc_cur = exact_score_unit(current)
    best_a = current.copy(); best_sc=sc_cur
    step=0
    while time<deadline:
       improved=False
       for mode, param, mix in modes:
          c=conv(current)
          p=make_weights(c, mode, param, mix)
          # solve
          x = solve_hankel(p,current,alpha)
          cand = project_solution(x,current)
          score_cand=exact_score(cand)
          # line search
          best_cand=current; best_trial_sc=sc_cur
          for lam in [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
             trial = normalize_pos(current + lam*(cand-current))
             sc_t=exact_score_unit(trial)
             if sc_t < best_trial_sc:
                best_trial_sc=sc_t; best_cand=trial
          if best_trial_sc < sc_cur - 1e-12:
             current=best_cand; sc_cur=best_trial_sc; improved=True
          elif best_trial_sc < sc_cur + 1e-10:
             # accept small/no change maybe to move? only if lower power_obj?
             pass
       if not improved:
          # decrease alpha/mix? reorder modes; break after cycles
          alpha *= decay? global
          if cycles>... break
```
But we need update alpha inside modes. Simpler: define a fixed schedule list of tuples (mode,param,mix,alpha,maxiter,iolve_steps). Run through schedule once or repeated. At each step, if no improvement after solving, lower alpha? We can pass alpha in schedule.

Algorithm:
```
def kkt_search(a0, deadline, max_candidates):
   base=normalize(a0)
   best_score=score(base)
   best_a=base
   schedules=...
   for schedule in schedules:

def perturb(a, rng, scale):
   n=len(a); x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   r=rng.random()
   if r<0.3: a_p = a*(1+scale*rng.standard_normal(n))
   elif r<0.6: freq=1+5*rng.random(); amp=scale*(0.5+rng.random()); a_p=a*(1+amp*np.cos(2π freq x+phase))
   elif: block scale 5-10 blocks
   else: mirror shift?

Implementation of `kkt_search`:
```
def kkt_search(a0, deadline, label='', max_schedules=None):
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize_pos(a0)
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_sc=exact_score_unit(a)
    cur_sc=best_sc
    cur_a=a.copy()
    schedules=default_kkt_schedules(n)
    if max_schedules: schedules=schedules[:max_schedules]
    no_improve=0
    for idx,(mode,param,mix,alpha0,maxiter,steps) in enumerate(schedules):
        cur_a=best_a.copy()
        for step in range(steps):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: return best_sc,best_a
            c=conv_full(cur_a)
            p=make_weights(c, mode, param, n, mix)
            alpha=max(1e-6, alpha0*(0.75**step))
            x=solve_kkt(p, cur_a, alpha, maxiter)
            if x is None: continue
            cand_list=project_solution(x, cur_a)
            improved=False
            for cand in cand_list:
                for lam in (1.0,0.7,0.4,0.2,0.1):
                    trial=blend_normalize(cur_a,cand,lam)
                    sc=exact_score_unit(trial)
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                       best_sc=sc; best_a=trial.copy(); improved=True
                    if sc < cur_sc:
                       cur_sc=sc; cur_a=trial.copy()
            if improved:
                cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
                no_improve=0
            else:
                no_improve+=1
                if no_improve>=3: break
        if no_improve>=6 and idx>3: break
        no_improve=0
    return best_sc,best_a
```
This accepts any trial better than current even if not global, to follow path; final best global. Good.

`project_solution` returns list of normalized candidates from x.
```
def project_solution(x, prev):
   arrs=[]
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return []
   pos=np.maximum(x,0)
   if pos.sum()>1e-12: arrs.append(pos)
   negvals=x[x<0]
   if len(negvals)>0:
      shift1=x-negvals.min()
      if shift1.sum()>1e-12: arrs.append(np.maximum(shift1,0))
      shift2=x-negvals.mean()
      if shift2.sum()>1e-12: arrs.append(np.maximum(shift2,0))
   # maybe add blend with prev before normalize? line search handles; but return base
   out=[]
   seen? not needed.
   for arr in arrs:
      norm=normalize_pos(arr)
      # if sum okay
      out.append(norm)
   # also prev? no
   return out
```

`solve_kkt` with LSQR:
```
def solve_kkt(p,a_prev,alpha,maxiter):
    n=len(a_prev)
    h=HankelOp(p,n)
    op=AugHankelOp(h,alpha,n,a_prev)
    L=2*n
    Aop=LinearOperator((L,n), op.matvec, rmatvec=op.rmatvec, dtype=np.float64)
    b=np.empty(L)
    b[:n]=1.0
    if alpha>0: b[n:]=np.sqrt(alpha)*a_prev
    else: b[n:]=0
    try:
       x,info=lsqr(Aop,b,atol=1e-7,btol=1e-7,iter_lim=maxiter)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return None
       return x
    except Exception:
       return None
```
Potential issue: LSQR's default `use_simple_transpose` may ignore `rmatvec`? It has parameter `use_simple_transpose` default False? If false, uses A^T via rmatvec if given. Good.

Could LSQR be slow due 2n length; maybe use `calc_var=False`. Add options: `lsqr(Aop, b, atol=1e-7, btol=1e-7, iter_lim=maxiter, calc_var=False)`. In older scipy? calc_var available. Use try.

`AugHankelOp.matvec`:
```
def matvec(self,v):
   av=self.h.matvec(v)
   if self.sqrt_alpha==0:
      av=np.concatenate((av, np.zeros(self.n)))
   else:
      av=np.concatenate((av, self.sqrt_alpha*v))
   return av
```

Now soft LSE objective class. Need avoid computing softmax twice (value and gradient). `soft_obj_core`:
```
def soft_obj_core(x, beta, x0):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    xmax=np.max(x)
    y=x-xmax
    a=np.exp(y)
    a=a/a.sum()
    c=conv_full(a) # sum1
    cmax=np.max(c)
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    scaled=np.clip(scaled, -700, 0)
    e=np.exp(scaled)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e10, zeros
    p=e/s
    L=cmax+np.log(s)/beta
    val=2*n*L + 1e-8*np.sum((x-x0)**2)
    # gradient
    G=2.0*hankel_apply(p,a)
    # G should be nonneg
    ag=np.dot(a,G)
    grad=G - a*ag + 2e-8*(x-x0)
    # maybe scale? 
    return val, grad
```
But note: `hankel_apply` computes p length L with a length n, output length n. It uses FFT. In objective, c computed, then hankel. Good.
Use `n=len(a)` from x. Need pass n? `x.size`.

If beta very small, log(s)/beta large? But value computed; gradient p uniform, G=2∑p a' ~? At beta→0, p=1/L; G constant row sum maybe not constant; gradient of variance term. Good.

Let's detail functions:

`conv_full(a)`:
```
def conv_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    m = 2*n - 1
    M = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
If a has tiny negative? normalized clips. Good.

`hankel_apply(p,a)`:
```
def hankel_apply(p,a):
    n=len(a); L=len(p) # should 2n-1
    need=L+n-1
    M=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fa,M)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
Check if p length not 2n-1? We'll always pass 2n-1. If not, need adjust? use len.

`exact_score_unit(a)`: assumes normalized but clip/normalize? To avoid changing in score, we can normalize copy for safety. But in loops, normalized. Use:
```
def exact_score_unit(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return np.inf
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12: a=a/s
    c=conv_full(a)
    return 2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(c))
```
This normalizes each call; okay but copies. In heavy loops, use `score_norm` to avoid renormalize? We can keep normalized; but safety small. For performance, define:
```
def score_norm(a):
   c=conv_full(a); return 2*n*max(c)
```
Assumes sum1. Use after normalize. In exact_score_unit for candidates. In loops use score_norm.

`normalize_pos(a)`:
```
def normalize_pos(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300:
       return np.full_like(a,1.0/max(1,len(a)))
    return a/s
```
If all zero, uniform.

`blend_normalize(a,b,lam)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a); b=np.asarray(b)
t=(1-lam)*a+lam*b
return normalize_pos(t)
```

`make_weights(c, mode, param, n, mix=0.0)`:
```
def make_weights(c, mode, param, n, mix=0.0):
    L=len(c); c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cm=float(np.max(c))
    if cm <=0: w=np.ones(L); w/=L; return w
    if mode == 'lse':
       beta = float(param) * max(1,n)
       scaled = beta*(c-cm)
       scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
       w = np.exp(scaled)
    elif mode == 'power':
       q = float(param)
       r = c/cm
       logw = q*np.log(np.maximum(r,1e-300))
       logw = np.clip(logw, -700.0, 0.0)
       w = np.exp(logw)
    elif mode == 'top':
       k = int(param)
       if k <=0 or k >= L:
          w=np.ones(L)
       else:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, L-k)[-k:]
          w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0
    else:
       w=np.ones(L)
    sw=float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
       w=np.ones(L); sw=L
    else: w = w/sw
    if mix > 0:
       m=float(mix)
       w = (1-m)*w + m*(1.0/L)
       sw=w.sum()
       if sw>0: w/=sw
    return w
```
If param lse scaled by n; schedule uses param numeric. For power, q param. For top, param int.

`default_kkt_schedules(n)`:

def default_kkt_schedules(n):
    return [
      ('power', 1.0, 0.5, 0.5, 80, 8),
      ('power', 1.0, 0.25, 0.1, 100, 8),
      ('power', 2.0, 0.25, 0.1, 100, 8),
      ('power', 4.0, 0.15, 0.05, 100, 8),
      ('lse', 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 100, 8),
      ('lse', 2.0, 0.15, 0.05, 100, 8),
      ('lse', 10.0, 0.05, 0.02, 120, 6),
      ('top', 100, 0.2, 0.05, 80, 5),
      ('top', 30, 0.1, 0.02, 80, 5),
    ]
```

`soft_lbfgs`:
```
def soft_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun=5000, maxiter=1000):
   a=normalize_pos(a)
   n=len(a)
   if n <=0: return score,a
   a_safe=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
   x0=np.log(a_safe)
   x0-=x0.max()
   obj=SoftObj(beta, x0)
   bounds=[(-50.0,50.0)] * n
   options={'maxiter':max(50,maxiter), 'maxfun':max(100,maxfun), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':50}
   try:
       res=minimize(obj, x0, jac=obj, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
       x=res.x
   except Exception:
       x=x0
   # ensure finite
   xf=np.asarray(x,float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(xf)): xf=x0
   xf=np.clip(xf, -50, 50)
   y=xf-xf.max()
   a_out=np.exp(y); s=a_out.sum(); if s<=0: a_out=np.ones(n)/n else a_out/=s
   sc=score_norm(a_out)
   return sc,a_out
```
`SoftObj.__call__` calls `soft_obj_core`. `soft_obj_core` uses `score`? It computes LSE. It also uses `x0` for regularization. Need x0 passed.
```
class SoftObj:
    def __init__(self,beta,x0): self.beta=float(beta); self.x0=np.asarray(x0,float)
    def __call__(self,x): return soft_obj_core(x,self.beta,self.x0)
```
`soft_obj_core` uses `n=x.size`.
Potential issue: `minimize` may pass `x` as shape (n,1)? It passes 1D. Fine.
If objective returns NaN, L-BFGS may fail; we return large.

`soft_obj_core` hankel apply: We compute Fp each eval; maybe we can avoid creating p array? p is exp scaled. We already have e sum p. But p needed for FFT. Use `p = e / s`. Good.
`c = conv_full(a)` computes FFT; `hankel_apply` computes another Fp and ra. Total transforms: conv: rfft a + irfft. hankel: rfft p + rfft a_rev + irfft. But a_rev rfft can maybe reuse rfft a? FFT of reverse not simply conjugate of a? Could compute from a? For real a, FFT(rev a) with zero padding not simply. Fine.

Could combine p and a? no.

`perturbation(a, rng, scale)`: top-level.
```
def perturbation(a,rng,scale):
    n=len(a); x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    r=rng.random()
    if r<0.25:
       ap=a*(1.0+scale*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif r<0.5:
       freq=1.0+8.0*rng.random(); phase=2*np.pi*rng.random(); amp=scale*(0.5+rng.random())
       ap=a*(1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+phase))
    elif r<0.75:
       ap=a.copy(); nb=3+int(rng.integers(0,8)); block=max(1,n//nb)
       for b in range(min(nb, n//block)):
           s=b*block; e=min(s+block,n); ap[s:e]*=(1.0+scale*2*(rng.random()-0.5))
    else:
       ap=a.copy(); ns=n//5; sel=rng.choice(n, size=ns, replace=False)
       ap[sel]*=(1.0+scale*3*(rng.random(ns)-0.5))
    return normalize_pos(ap)
```
Need `rng.integers` returns np.int; convert int.

`resample_seq(a,n)`:
```
def resample_seq(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); if a.size==0...
    if a.size==n: return normalize_pos(a)
    old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    b=np.interp(new, old, a)
    return normalize_pos(b)
```

Pseudo:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(120,float(budget_s))
    deadline=start+budget-90
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if p is not None:
          p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
          if p.size>=64 and np.isfinite(p).all():
             parent=normalize_pos(p)
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is not None:
       best_a=parent.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(best_a); print
    else:
       best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    n_main=8192
    cands=initial_candidates(n_main,parent,rng)
    if parent is not None:
       add_candidate(cands,parent)
    cands.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    if not cands:
       best_a=np.ones(1024)/1024; best_sc=score_norm(best_a)
    else:
       # ensure best from candidates too
       for sc,a in cands:
          if sc<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=normalize_pos(a)
       if best_a is None: best_a=cands[0][1]
    # KKT on top
    top=cands[:10]
    chunks=[240,180,180,120,120,90,90,60,60,60]
    for i,(sc,a) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic()>deadline-600: break
        if len(a)>20000: continue
        chunk=chunks[i] if i<len(chunks) else 45
        local_deadline=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+chunk)
        print(...)
        sc_k,a_k=kkt_search(a, local_deadline, label=f'init{i}', max_schedules=8)
        if sc_k<best_sc: best_sc=sc_k; best_a=a_k.copy()
    # L-BFGS
    if len(best_a)>20000: best_a=resample_seq(best_a,16384); best_sc=score_norm(best_a)
    for bs in [0.2,0.5,1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0,100.0]:
        if time.monotonic()>deadline-240: break
        n=len(best_a)
        beta=bs*n
        remain=deadline-time.monotonic()
        maxfun=int(min(8000, max(1500, remain*2)))
        maxiter=max(200, maxfun//10)
        sc,a=soft_lbfgs(best_a,beta,maxfun,maxiter)
        if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    # ILS
    trial=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 300 and trial<25:
       n=len(best_a)
       scale=0.005+0.04*rng.random()
       ap=perturbation(best_a,rng,scale)
       local_deadline=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+120)
       sc,aq=kkt_search(ap, local_deadline, label=f'ILS{trial}', max_schedules=3)
       if time.monotonic() < local_deadline - 60:
          sc2,a2=soft_lbfgs(aq, 10*n, 2500, 500)
          if sc2<sc: sc,a=sc2,a2
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       trial+=1
    # length expl
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 500:
       for n_try in [16384,4096]:
          if time.monotonic()>deadline-350: break
          if len(best_a)==n_try: continue
          ar=resample_seq(best_a,n_try)
          local_deadline=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+300)
          sc,a=kkt_search(ar, local_deadline, label=f'len{n_try}', max_schedules=4)
          if time.monotonic() < local_deadline - 120:
             sc2,a2=soft_lbfgs(a, 10*n_try, 4000, 700)
             if sc2<sc: sc,a=sc2,a2
          if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    # final KKT
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       sc,a=kkt_search(best_a, deadline-30, label='final', max_schedules=4)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    # final cap length
    if best_a is None: best_a=np.ones(1024)/1024
    if len(best_a)>16384: best_a=resample_seq(best_a,16384); best_sc=score_norm(best_a)
    out=np.asarray(best_a,float)
    out=np.clip(out,0,1000)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<0.1: # if small? but sum1; if not
       out=np.ones(1024)*0.1
    # We don't rescale? sum1 okay. If resample produced sum1.
    out_list=[float(x) for x in out]
    # verify and compare parent
    final_actual=np.inf
    try: final_actual=float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception: final_actual=best_sc
    parent_actual=np.inf; parent_list=None
    if parent is not None:
       pout=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in parent]
       if sum(pout)>=0.01:
          try: parent_actual=float(evaluate_sequence(pout))
          except: parent_actual=best_sc
          if parent_actual<final_actual:
             out_list=pout; final_actual=parent_actual
    print final
    return out_list
```

Let's write classes:

```
class HankelOp:
    def __init__(self, p, n):
       self.n=n
       L=2*n-1
       need=L+n-1
       self.M=1 << (need-1).bit_length()
       self.Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,self.M)
    def matvec(self, v):
       n=self.n
       r=v[::-1]
       Fr=np.fft.rfft(r,self.M)
       out=np.fft.irfft(self.Fp*Fr,self.M)
       return np.maximum(out[n-1:2*n-1],0.0)
```
If `p` shape (L,) but maybe has small negative? make_weights nonnegative. Good.

`AugHankelOp`:
```
class AugHankelOp:
   def __init__(self,h,alpha,n,a_prev):
      self.h=h; self.n=n; self.alpha=float(alpha); self.sqrt_alpha=np.sqrt(self.alpha) if alpha>0 else 0.0
   def matvec(self,v):
      av=self.h.matvec(v)
      if self.sqrt_alpha == 0.0:
         return np.concatenate((av, np.zeros(self.n, dtype=np.float64)))
      return np.concatenate((av, self.sqrt_alpha * v))
   def rmatvec(self,w):
      n=self.n
      if n <=0: return np.zeros(0)
      w1 = w[:n]
      av = self.h.matvec(w1)
      if self.alpha == 0.0:
         return av
      w2=w[n:]
      return av + self.alpha * w2
```
In `rmatvec`, w2 shape (n,), alpha scalar. If w is length 2n. Good.

`solve_kkt` uses `op=AugHankelOp(h,alpha,n,a_prev); Aop=LinearOperator((2*n,n), op.matvec, rmatvec=op.rmatvec, dtype=np.float64)`.
Potential: `LinearOperator` may require shape tuple exactly; yes. `matvec` returns (2n,), `rmatvec` returns (n,). good.

LSQR `b` length 2n. If alpha=0 bottom zeros. Good.

Could LSQR complain if `rmatvec` not match r of `matvec`? It uses A^T v where v length 2n, returns n. yes.

One concern: `opt = minimize(obj, x0, jac=obj, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)`; `obj` is SoftObj instance; L-BFGS-B checks if jac is same? It can call `obj` as gradient. If `jac` is a callable, it is used for gradient; `fun` should return f only. If we pass same callable returning tuple as `jac`, it may break. We should use `jac=True` and `fun=obj`. L-BFGS-B with `jac=True` will use tuple returned. So call:
```
res = minimize(obj, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options, jac=True)
```
Yes. Or pass `fun=obj, jac=obj` but then fun should return f only; not. Use `jac=True`.

def kkt_search(a0, deadline, label='', max_schedules=None):
    a=normalize_pos(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(a); cur_a=a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
    schedules=default_kkt_schedules(len(a))
    if max_schedules is not None:
       schedules=schedules[:int(max_schedules)]
    no_improve=0
    for idx,sched in enumerate(schedules):
       cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
       mode,param,mix,alpha0,maxiter,steps=sched
       for step in range(int(steps)):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline:
             break
          c=conv_full(cur_a)
          p=make_weights(c,mode,param,len(cur_a),mix)
          alpha=max(1e-8, float(alpha0)*(0.75**step))
          x=solve_kkt(p,cur_a,alpha,maxiter)
          if x is None: continue
          cands=project_solution(x, cur_a)
          improved_cur=False
          improved_best=False
          for cand in cands:
             for lam in (1.0,0.7,0.4,0.2,0.1):
                trial=blend_normalize(cur_a,cand,lam)
                sc=score_norm(trial)
                if sc < cur_sc:
                   cur_a=trial.copy(); cur_sc=sc; improved_cur=True
                if sc < best_sc:
                   best_a=trial.copy(); best_sc=sc; improved_best=True
          if improved_best:
             cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
             no_improve=0
          elif improved_cur:
             no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve += 1
             if no_improve >= 3:
                break
       if idx > 0 and no_improve >= 6:
          break
    if best_sc < score_norm(a0): print?
    return best_sc, best_a
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2)

The code uses:
- FFT-based exact scoring for internal feedback
- a Bank/KKT-inspired linear solve: for a weight vector p on lags,
  solve approximately A(p) a = 1, where (A(p)a)_j = sum_t p_{j+t} a_t.
  This enforces local stationarity for weighted-power objectives sum c_k^q.
- softmax-parameterized log-sum-exp optimization of the peak via L-BFGS-B
- iterated local search, length rescaling, and final verification
"""

import time
import numpy as np
import operator
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.sparse.linalg import LinearOperator, lsqr
    _HAS_SPARSE = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SPARSE = False


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_pos(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def conv_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:m]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def hankel_apply(p, a):
    """
    Returns A(p) a, where (A(p)a)_j = sum_t p_{j+t} a_t.
    p has length 2n-1, a length n.
    """
    n = len(a)
    L = len(p)
    need = L + n - 1
    M = next_pow2(need)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    Fr = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], M)
    out = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fr, M)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def score_norm(a):
    """Exact score assuming sum(a)=1."""
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    c = conv_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))


def exact_score_any(a):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    return score_norm(a)


def blend_normalize(a, b, lam):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.shape != b.shape:
        b = resample_seq(b, len(a))
    t = (1.0 - float(lam)) * a + float(lam) * b
    return normalize_pos(t)


def resample_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(int(n), 1.0 / max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    if int(n) <= 0:
        return a
    if int(n) == a.size:
        return normalize_pos(a)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    b = np.interp(new, old, a)
    return normalize_pos(b)


def add_candidate(cands, a):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    if a.size < 2 or a.size > 30000:
        return
    sc = score_norm(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append((sc, a))


def make_weights(c, mode, param, n, mix=0.0):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    L = len(c)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cm):
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= L
        return w

    if mode == "lse":
        beta = float(param) * max(1, int(n))
        scaled = beta * (c - cm)
        scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(scaled)
    elif mode == "power":
        q = float(param)
        r = c / cm
        logw = q * np.log(np.maximum(r, 1e-300))
        logw = np.clip(logw, -700.0, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(logw)
    elif mode == "top":
        k = int(param)
        if k <= 0 or k >= L:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, L - k)[-k:]
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)

    sw = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        sw = float(L)
    else:
        w = w / sw

    if mix > 0.0:
        m = min(0.999, float(mix))
        w = (1.0 - m) * w + m * (1.0 / float(L))
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sw):
            w = w / sw
    return w


class HankelOp:
    def __init__(self, p, n):
        self.n = int(n)
        L = 2 * self.n - 1
        need = L + self.n - 1
        self.M = next_pow2(need)
        self.Fp = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), self.M)

    def matvec(self, v):
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        n = self.n
        r = v[::-1]
        Fr = np.fft.rfft(r, self.M)
        out = np.fft.irfft(self.Fp * Fr, self.M)
        return np.maximum(out[n - 1:2 * n - 1], 0.0)


class AugHankelOp:
    def __init__(self, h, alpha, n, a_prev):
        self.h = h
        self.n = int(n)
        self.alpha = float(alpha)
        self.sqrt_alpha = np.sqrt(self.alpha) if self.alpha > 0 else 0.0

    def matvec(self, v):
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        av = self.h.matvec(v)
        if self.sqrt_alpha == 0.0:
            return np.concatenate((av, np.zeros(self.n, dtype=np.float64)))
        return np.concatenate((av, self.sqrt_alpha * v))

    def rmatvec(self, w):
        w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if w.size <= 0:
            return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
        n = self.n
        w1 = w[:n]
        av = self.h.matvec(w1)
        if self.alpha == 0.0:
            return av
        w2 = w[n:]
        return av + self.alpha * w2


def solve_kkt(p, a_prev, alpha, maxiter):
    if not _HAS_SPARSE:
        return None
    n = len(a_prev)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    maxiter = int(min(maxiter, max(20, 800000 // max(1, n))))
    h = HankelOp(p, n)
    op = AugHankelOp(h, alpha, n, a_prev)
    Aop = LinearOperator((2 * n, n), matvec=op.matvec,
                         rmatvec=op.rmatvec, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.empty(2 * n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[:n] = 1.0
    sqrt_alpha = float(np.sqrt(alpha)) if alpha > 0 else 0.0
    b[n:] = sqrt_alpha * np.asarray(a_prev, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        x, info = lsqr(Aop, b, atol=1e-7, btol=1e-7,
                       iter_lim=maxiter, calc_var=False)
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return None
        return x
    except Exception:
        return None


def project_solution(x, prev):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return []
    raws = []

    pos = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    if float(pos.sum()) > 1e-12:
        raws.append(pos)

    neg = x[x < 0.0]
    shifts = []
    allmin = float(np.min(x))
    if allmin < 0.0:
        shifts.append(allmin)
    if neg.size:
        shifts.append(float(np.mean(neg)))
        if neg.size > 1:
            shifts.append(float(np.median(neg)))

    for sh in shifts:
        y = x - sh
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        if float(y.sum()) > 1e-12:
            raws.append(y)

    # Occasionally use a shifted version to make more mass positive.
    if len(raws) < 3:
        y = np.maximum(x - 0.25 * float(np.min(x)), 0.0)
        if float(y.sum()) > 1e-12:
            raws.append(y)

    out = []
    for arr in raws:
        arr = normalize_pos(arr)
        out.append(arr)
    return out


def default_kkt_schedules(n):
    # (mode, param, mix_uniform, alpha0, maxiter, steps)
    # param: power exponent, lse scale (times n), or top-k.
    return [
        ("power", 1.0, 0.50, 0.50, 60, 6),
        ("power", 1.0, 0.25, 0.20, 80, 6),
        ("power", 2.0, 0.20, 0.10, 80, 6),
        ("power", 4.0, 0.10, 0.05, 80, 5),
        ("lse",   1.0, 0.20, 0.10, 80, 5),
        ("lse",   5.0, 0.10, 0.05, 100, 5),
        ("top", 100.0, 0.20, 0.05, 70, 4),
        ("top",  30.0, 0.10, 0.02, 70, 4),
    ]


def kkt_search(a0, deadline, label="", max_schedules=None):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_pos(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_norm(a)
    start_sc = best_sc
    schedules = default_kkt_schedules(len(a))
    if max_schedules is not None:
        schedules = schedules[:int(max_schedules)]

    no_improve = 0
    for idx, sched in enumerate(schedules):
        cur_a = best_a.copy()
        cur_sc = best_sc
        mode, param, mix, alpha0, maxiter, steps = sched
        for step in range(int(steps)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            c = conv_full(cur_a)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
                break
            p = make_weights(c, mode, param, len(cur_a), mix)
            alpha = max(1e-8, float(alpha0) * (0.75 ** step))
            x = solve_kkt(p, cur_a, alpha, maxiter)
            if x is None:
                continue
            cands = project_solution(x, cur_a)
            improved_cur = False
            improved_best = False
            for cand in cands:
                for lam in (1.0, 0.7, 0.4, 0.2, 0.1):
                    trial = blend_normalize(cur_a, cand, lam)
                    sc = score_norm(trial)
                    if not np.isfinite(sc):
                        continue
                    if sc < cur_sc:
                        cur_a = trial.copy()
                        cur_sc = sc
                        improved_cur = True
                    if sc < best_sc:
                        best_a = trial.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
                        improved_best = True

            if improved_best:
                cur_a = best_a.copy()
                cur_sc = best_sc
                no_improve = 0
            elif improved_cur:
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 3:
                    break

        if idx > 0 and no_improve >= 6:
            break

    if label:
        print(f"  KKT {label}: {start_sc:.10f} -> {best_sc:.10f} "
              f"(n={len(a)}, {time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s)")
    return best_sc, best_a


def soft_obj_core(x, beta, x0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    y = x - float(np.max(x))
    ea = np.exp(np.clip(y, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(ea.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = ea / s

    c = conv_full(a)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    scaled = beta * (c - cm)
    scaled = np.clip(scaled, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(es):
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
    else:
        p = e / es

    if float(np.max(p)) <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones_like(c) / float(c.size)

    L = cm + float(np.log(max(es, 1e-300))) / beta if beta > 0 else cm
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * L
    reg = 1e-8
    val += reg * float(np.sum((x - x0) ** 2))

    G = 2.0 * hankel_apply(p, a)
    ag = float(np.dot(a, G))
    grad = G - a * ag + (2.0 * reg) * (x - x0)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(val), grad


class SoftObj:
    def __init__(self, beta, x0):
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self.x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()

    def __call__(self, x):
        return soft_obj_core(x, self.beta, self.x0)


def soft_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun=5000, maxiter=1000):
    a = normalize_pos(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf, a
    if n > 30000:
        a = resample_seq(a, 20000)
        n = len(a)

    a_safe = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    x0 = np.log(a_safe)
    x0 -= float(np.max(x0))
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -45.0, 45.0)
    x0 -= float(np.max(x0))
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -45.0, 45.0)

    maxfun = int(min(maxfun, max(1000, 50000000 // max(1, n))))
    maxiter = int(max(200, maxfun // 10))

    obj = SoftObj(beta, x0)
    bounds = [(-50.0, 50.0)] * n
    options = {
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxls": 50
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(obj, x0, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True,
                       bounds=bounds, options=options)
        x = res.x
    except Exception:
        x = x0

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = x0
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    ea = np.exp(np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(ea.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a_out = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a_out = ea / s
    a_out = normalize_pos(a_out)
    return score_norm(a_out), a_out


def initial_candidates(n, parent, rng):
    cands = []
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    add_candidate(cands, np.ones(n))

    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        add_candidate(cands, resample_seq(parent, n))

    for rate in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0]:
        add_candidate(cands, np.exp(rate * x))
        add_candidate(cands, np.exp(-rate * x))

    xs = np.linspace(1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6, n)
    for al in [0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0]:
        for be in [0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0]:
            add_candidate(cands, xs ** al * (1.0 - xs) ** be)

    for center in [0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.80, 0.90]:
        for width in [0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40]:
            a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - center) / width) ** 2)
            add_candidate(cands, a)

    for _ in range(12):
        r = 1e-6
        for _ in range(4):
            freq = 1.0 + 10.0 * rng.random()
            phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
            amp = 0.1 + 0.6 * rng.random()
            r = r * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))
        add_candidate(cands, np.abs(r) + 0.05)

    cands.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    return cands


def perturbation(a, rng, scale):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    r = rng.random()

    if r < 0.25:
        ap = a * (1.0 + float(scale) * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif r < 0.50:
        freq = 1.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        amp = float(scale) * (0.5 + rng.random())
        ap = a * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))
    elif r < 0.75:
        ap = a.copy()
        nb = 3 + int(rng.integers(0, 8))
        block = max(1, n // nb)
        for b in range(min(nb, max(1, n // block))):
            s = b * block
            e = min(s + block, n)
            ap[s:e] *= (1.0 + float(scale) * 2.0 * (rng.random() - 0.5))
    else:
        ap = a.copy()
        ns = max(1, n // 5)
        sel = rng.choice(n, size=ns, replace=False)
        ap[sel] *= (1.0 + float(scale) * 3.0 * (rng.random(ns) - 0.5))

    return normalize_pos(ap)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(300.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, budget - 180.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if p is not None:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = normalize_pos(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        best_a = parent.copy()
        best_sc = score_norm(best_a)
        print(f"Parent: n={len(best_a)}, internal score={best_sc:.10f}")

    n_main = 8192
    cands = initial_candidates(n_main, parent, rng)
    if parent is not None:
        add_candidate(cands, parent)
    if cands:
        cands.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    else:
        fallback = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_a = fallback
        best_sc = score_norm(fallback)
        cands = [(best_sc, fallback)]

    if best_a is None:
        best_sc, best_a = cands[0]

    for sc, a in cands[:80]:
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_pos(a)

    print(f"After initial batch: n={len(best_a)}, score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # KKT-style searches from several diverse starts.
    top = cands[:10]
    chunks = [240, 180, 180, 120, 120, 90, 90, 60, 60, 60]
    for i, (sc, a) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 600:
            break
        if a.size > 20000:
            continue
        chunk = chunks[i] if i < len(chunks) else 45
        ld = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + chunk)
        sc_k, a_k = kkt_search(a, ld, label=f"init{i}", max_schedules=8)
        if sc_k < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_k
            best_a = normalize_pos(a_k)

    # L-BFGS refinements with increasing LSE beta.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size > 20000:
        best_a = resample_seq(best_a, 16384)
        best_sc = score_norm(best_a)

    for bs in [0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 100.0]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240:
            break
        n = len(best_a)
        beta = float(bs) * float(n)
        remain = max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        maxfun = int(min(8000, max(1500, remain * 2)))
        maxiter = int(max(200, maxfun // 10))
        sc, a = soft_lbfgs(best_a, beta, maxfun=maxfun, maxiter=maxiter)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_pos(a)

    # Iterated local search.
    trial = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 300 and trial < 25:
        n = len(best_a)
        scale = 0.005 + 0.04 * rng.random()
        ap = perturbation(best_a, rng, scale)
        ld = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 120)
        sc, aq = kkt_search(ap, ld, label=f"ILS{trial}", max_schedules=3)
        if time.monotonic() < ld - 60:
            sc2, a2 = soft_lbfgs(aq, 10.0 * len(aq), maxfun=2500, maxiter=500)
            if sc2 < sc:
                sc, aq = sc2, a2
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_pos(aq)
        trial += 1

    # Length exploration, if enough time.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 500:
        for n_try in [16384, 4096]:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 350:
                break
            if len(best_a) == n_try:
                continue
            ar = resample_seq(best_a, n_try)
            ld = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 300)
            sc, a = kkt_search(ar, ld, label=f"len{n_try}", max_schedules=4)
            if time.monotonic() < ld - 120:
                sc2, a2 = soft_lbfgs(a, 10.0 * n_try, maxfun=4000, maxiter=700)
                if sc2 < sc:
                    sc, a = sc2, a2
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_pos(a)

    # Final KKT polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        sc, a = kkt_search(best_a, deadline - 30, label="final", max_schedules=4)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_pos(a)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    # Safety cap for final verify cost.
    if best_a.size > 16384:
        best_a = resample_seq(best_a, 16384)
        best_sc = score_norm(best_a)

    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 0.1:
        out = np.ones(max(64, len(out)), dtype=np.float64)

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out]

    final_actual = np.inf
    try:
        final_actual = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        final_actual = best_sc

    parent_actual = np.inf
    parent_list = None
    if parent is not None:
        pout = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(x)))) for x in parent]
        if pout and sum(pout) >= 0.01:
            try:
                parent_actual = float(evaluate_sequence(pout))
            except Exception:
                parent_actual = np.inf
            if parent_actual < final_actual:
                out_list = pout
                final_actual = parent_actual

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f}, actual_verified={final_actual:.12f}")
    print(f"Length={len(out_list)}, elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s / {budget:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return out_list
```
